#### T1 — Terminal launcher

- **Change:** `navo-app/src/terminal/mod.rs`: `fn command_line(provider: &str, template: Option<&str>, folder: &Path) -> Result<(String, Vec<String>), String>`. Providers: `windows-terminal` → `wt.exe -d <folder>`; `powershell` → `pwsh.exe` (fallback `powershell.exe`) with `-NoExit -WorkingDirectory <folder>`; `cmd` → `cmd.exe /K cd /d <folder>`; `custom` → the template split like a Windows command line with `{path}` replaced. On Linux: `$TERMINAL` or `x-terminal-emulator`, working directory set. `fn hooks() -> TerminalHooks` spawns with `current_dir(folder)`.

Command `files.open_terminal` ("Open terminal here"), no default key (Q10). A non-local folder shows "A terminal opens only in a local folder." in the status line.
- **Check:** a window test with a fake hook receives the active folder; the error shows in the status line.

#### T3 — Terminal entries and wiring

- **Change:** "Open terminal here" in the empty-space menu after "Copy folder path"; `gui.rs` passes `terminal::hooks()`.
- **Check:** shell menu tests list the entry; the app crate builds.

#### K1 — Cache sources

- **Change:** register "Ctrl+K index" (sum of the index files the service or the app writes) and "cache.db on disk" (file plus WAL size) in `CacheRegistry`. Add `pub fn cache_rows(&CacheRegistry) -> Vec<CacheRow { name, bytes, entries, budget }>` for the UI hook.
- **Check:** tests with a temp profile: both rows appear with non-zero sizes after a write; a missing index file shows 0.

#### K2 — Diagnostics page

- **Change:** `Section::Diagnostics` ("Diagnostics") with a read-only page like the Search page: one row per cache (name, size, entries, budget), a Refresh button, and a link to "Save a diagnostic report". `SettingsHooks.caches` defaults to an empty list.
- **Check:** settings tests: the section lists the rows from a fake hook; Refresh calls it again.

#### K3 — Cache stress test

- **Change:** an ignored test pushes 50,000 folder listings through the disk cache with the default budget, then checks the used bytes stay under the budget and the in-memory stats match `cache_db.usage`.
- **Check:** runs under 60 s in release on CI; the milestone gate runs it once with `--run-ignored only`.

#### X0 — UIA probe (Red)

- **Change:** a Windows CI step starts `navo.exe --profile <temp>` with a test folder, then a PowerShell script with `UIAutomationClient` dumps the tree to the job log and asserts: the file list has `DataItem` or `ListItem` children with non-empty names; F8 opens a dialog with a name and named buttons; Ctrl+K results have names. The report explains the D2 finding with evidence (AccessKit adapter not started, missing names, or no focus events).

#### X7 — Contrast themes

- **Change:** when Windows reports a contrast theme (`SPI_GETHIGHCONTRAST`), the theme takes window, text, highlight, button and grey text colours from the system and turns off shadows and translucency. Leaving the contrast theme restores the user's theme.
- **Check:** token tests with a fake system palette; every text pair passes 7:1 in `contrast.mjs` terms.
- **Stop if:** GPUI does not notify a system colour change; report it and fall back to a check at start-up.

#### X8 — UI scale

- **Change:** `appearance.ui-scale` (percent, 80–200, default 100) multiplies text sizes, spacing, radii and icon sizes in the theme tokens. It applies live, like the font size. Settings > Appearance shows it as a number with steps of 10.
- **Check:** token tests at 80, 100 and 150 %; an out-of-range value shows the store's error.

## B7 · Find and collect

D19 (owner pick D82, direction A of `.crew/design/find-files/index.html`). Saved column sets stay in B7 but are not atomised here; they follow after G16, with their open question: does a saved set belong to a folder, a pane or a workspace?

What exists (checked at `c016ee35`, paths under `crates/`): Ctrl+K parses `>` (commands), `/` (files) and `ext:` (`navo-ui/src/command_center/query.rs:1-77`); sources are `ResultSource`s with `Group`, `Hit` and `HitSink` (`command_center/source.rs:12-160`); the input chip exists (`command_center/view/chip.rs`). In Ctrl+K, Tab and Shift+Tab jump between result groups (`commands/keymap.toml:644-660`) and Ctrl+Enter opens in the other pane (`keymap.toml:686-694`). The viewer already finds text and hex in UTF-8 and UTF-16 with `Needle` and an internal `Matcher` (`navo-core/src/paging/search.rs:1-262`); `navo-core` has `regex`, `aho-corasick`, `memchr` and `encoding_rs`. The folder walk for sizes is `navo-fs/src/measure/walk.rs:11-120` (`Visit`, `Walk`). I/O pools are `JobKind { Listing, Prefetch, Preview, Size, Hash, Module }` (`navo-fs/src/io_queue/pools.rs:6-44`). The viewer opens at a line (`navo-ui/src/window_view/viewer/mod.rs:227`). Archives list and read entries (`navo-fs/src/archive/mod.rs:21-29,166-186`). Multi-rename presets show the `navo.db` table pattern (`navo-store/src/db/rename_presets.rs`).

### Decisions for B7

- D19a — **Results are a virtual folder of relative paths.** A results tab has the location `find:<id>` for display. Its entries' names are paths relative to a base folder (`src\can\can_tx.c`); the base is the search scope, or for a collection the deepest common folder. Every action that joins a tab folder and an entry name uses the tab's base instead, so F3, F5, F6, rename, drag and the status bar act on the real files. A collection that spans drives uses the local root as base and absolute names. G0 lists the join sites first.
- D19b — **Content search walks the folders each time** (Q19); there is no content index in the beta. It runs in a new `JobKind::Find` pool (SSD 2, HDD 1, network 1, removable 1), one search per tab, cancelled when the tab closes.
- D19c — **The palette's first hits are a bounded search:** 300 ms after typing stops, at most 20 hits or 2 s, cancelled by the next key.

Order: G0, G1 and G12 start at once. G1 → G2 → G3 → G4, G6. G0 → G5 → G6, G9. Q18 → G10. G6 + G10 → G7 → G8, G13. G2 + G10 → G11. G13 → G14. G17a runs at the end of the beta; G17b only if it wins. G18a runs after the speed phase, with KS4; G18b only if it wins.

#### G1 — Find query

- **Change:** new `navo-core/src/find/mod.rs` and `find/query.rs`. Words are the text (quotes keep a phrase). Tokens: `ext:c,h,map` (list); `size:<50MB`, `size:>1k`, `size:1k..2M` (B, k, M, G; binary units); `mod:<7d`, `mod:>2026-10-01`, `mod:today`; `attr:hidden,readonly,system,archive` and `-hidden` to exclude; `in:<folder>` (relative to the scope or absolute; overrides the scope); `arc:on|off`; `re:<pattern>` (the rest of the token is a regex). `case` and `regex` are flags set by Alt+C and Alt+R. Each token keeps its byte span and, when invalid, a message ("size: needs a number, like <50MB").
- **Check:** tests per token; spans; invalid tokens keep the rest of the query; an empty text with only filters is valid (lists files without content matching).

#### G2 — Content matcher

- **Change:** new `navo-core/src/find/matcher.rs`. `Compiled::new(&FindQuery)`: literal text through `memchr`/`aho-corasick`, case-insensitive unless `case`; regex through `regex` with the same case rule. `scan(reader, &Compiled, cancel) -> FileHits { hits: Vec<LineHit { line: u64, column: u32, text: String }>, total: u32, skipped: Option<Skip> }`. Encodings: UTF-16 LE or BE with a BOM; UTF-8; a line that is not UTF-8 decodes as Windows-1252. A file is binary, and skipped, when its first 8 KiB hold a NUL byte and it has no UTF-16 BOM. Reads in 1 MiB chunks and carries partial lines. At most 1,000 hits per file are kept (`total` counts all); hit text is cut to 200 characters around the match.
- **Check:** tests: UTF-8, UTF-16 LE with BOM, a 1252 line with "é", CRLF line numbers, a match across a chunk border, a binary file skipped, regex and case flags, cancel stops early.

#### G3 — Find walk

- **Change:** new `navo-fs/src/find/` (`mod.rs`, `walk.rs`). Walks the base folder depth-first on the local provider (other providers refuse with "Search inside files works on local and network drives."), skipping hidden and system folders unless `attr:` asks for them, and `.git`. Filters by `ext`, `size`, `modified`, `attrs` before reading. Files larger than 64 MB are skipped unless `size:` allows them (Q19). Reads run in a new `JobKind::Find` pool; results stream as `FoundBatch` every 100 ms or 200 files, sorted by folder within a batch; progress gives files scanned, hits, files with hits, the current path. `CancelToken` stops within one chunk.
- **Check:** tests on a temp tree: filters, a hit in a nested folder with its relative path, binary skipped, cancel, progress counts.

- **G0 result (2026-10-27, `7611026a`):** sites that join the tab folder and an entry name, all in `navo-ui/src/`: `browser/open.rs:29` (Enter), `browser/operate/mod.rs:243` (F5/F6/F8 sources), `window_view/viewer/mod.rs:216` (F3), `window_view/editor.rs:39` (F4), `browser/naming/mod.rs:89` (rename), `window_view/drop.rs:218` (drag onto a row), `window_view/shell_menu/targets.rs:25` (context menu), `window_view/preview/follow.rs:74` (preview), `browser/prefetch/mod.rs:103` (folder prefetch), `window_view/multi_rename/gather.rs:75` (`parent: folder`). `browser/measure.rs:90` takes its folder from `browser/mod.rs:301`. No site in `status_bar.rs`. G5 runs in three parts of at most 3 files: G5a core `Tab::base` + open, operate, viewer; G5b editor, naming, drop, shell menu; G5c preview, prefetch, multi-rename gather, measure caller.

- **Decided 2026-10-27 (G5a stop):** `Location::join` cannot put a drive under the local root (`/` + `C:` gives `/C:/…`). So the join helper treats an absolute entry name (`X:\…`, `\\server\…`) as a full path: it builds it with `Location::new(name)` and ignores the base. A collection that spans drives keeps the local root as its displayed base and absolute names, as D19a says; nothing joins onto `/`.

- **Decided 2026-10-27 (G5a second stop):** `Tab::base` is runtime-only. Sessions are Postcard v3, so a new field needs a versioned migration; that is atom G5d after G7, when results tabs exist. Until then a results tab is not restored after a restart.

#### G4 — Inside archives

- **Change:** with `arc:on`, zip, 7z and tar files in the walk are opened with the archive readers and their entries matched like files; their relative path is `archive.zip\inner\file.c`. Nested archives are not entered. Entries over 64 MB are skipped.
- **Check:** tests: a hit inside a zip; `arc:off` skips it; a broken archive counts as skipped, not as an error.

#### G5 — Results base

- **Change:** `Tab` gains `base: Option<Location>`; `None` means the tab's folder. The sites G0 lists use `tab.base_or_location()` to join entry names, at most 3 files per run (the orchestrator splits G5 if G0 lists more files). Relative names with separators join segment by segment.
- **Check:** tests: a tab with base `C:\w` and entry `src\a.c` resolves to `C:\w\src\a.c` at every listed site; ordinary tabs unchanged.
- **Stop if:** `Location::join` cannot express a drive-rooted name from the local root; report.

#### G6 — Results model

- **Change:** `navo-ui/src/browser/find.rs`: `Browser::start_find(pane, query, base) -> TabId` opens a tab with location `find:<id>` and base, starts G3 under the tab's pool owner, and appends each batch to the tab's listing as entries with relative names; `stop_find(tab)` cancels and keeps what was found; `rerun_find(tab)` (Ctrl+R) clears and starts again. Hits per file are kept beside the listing for G8.
- **Check:** tests with a fake walk: batches append; stop keeps entries; closing the tab cancels; rerun replaces.

#### G7 — Results tab

- **Change:** Ctrl+Enter in Content mode closes Ctrl+K and calls `start_find` in the active pane. The tab label is the query text with a search icon; the path bar shows "search: <text> · <filters>"; a thin progress edge under it runs while searching; the status bar shows "Searching · N hits in M files · Esc stop" and then "N hits in M files". Esc in the file list stops a running search. A Name column shows the file name; a Folder column shows its relative folder.
- **Check:** tests: Ctrl+Enter opens the tab; the label and path text; Esc stops; the status text after the end.

#### G8 — Hit lines

- **Change:** under each file row of a results tab, up to two hit lines (line number, text with the match highlighted, mono, indented); "+N more" when there are more. Right on a file expands all its hits; Left collapses. Up and Down move over files; Ctrl+Down enters the hit lines of the current file. F3 on a file opens the viewer at its first hit; F3 on a hit line opens it at that line.
- **Check:** tests: two lines shown, "+3 more"; Right expands; F3 on a hit line opens at that line.

#### G9 — Copy from results

- **Change:** when the source tab has a base and an entry name has separators, the F5/F6 dialog adds a choice "Keep folder structure" (default on). On: the target gets `src\can\` folders created as needed. Off: files land flat; name collisions between sources go through the existing conflict flow and are never overwritten silently.
- **Check:** op plan tests: structure kept creates folders; flat with two `a.c` from different folders asks once per collision; a window test shows the choice only for results tabs.

#### G10 — Palette modes

- **Change:** a mode row under the Ctrl+K input: Names, Content, Commands, Collections; the current mode is pressed. Mode keys per Q18 (default: Tab and Shift+Tab switch modes; group jumps move to Ctrl+Down and Ctrl+Up). Alt+F7 in the file list opens Ctrl+K in Content mode with the active pane's folder as scope, shown as an `in:` chip. In Names mode the last row reads "Search inside files for <text> in <folder>" and Enter on it switches to Content mode. The `>` prefix still selects Commands.
- **Check:** tests: Tab cycles modes; Alt+F7 opens Content with the scope chip; Ctrl+Down jumps groups; snapshots show the moved and new keys.

#### G11 — Content mode

- **Change:** a `ContentSource` for Content mode: the input parses with `FindQuery`; tokens render as chips (invalid ones in the error colour with their message as a tooltip); Alt+C and Alt+R toggle case and regex chips. First hits per D19c show as rows "line text · file:line". Enter on a hit opens the viewer at its line. The footer shows "Enter open hit · Ctrl+Enter results to pane · Alt+R regex · Alt+C case". Ctrl+Enter in Names mode keeps "other pane".
- **Check:** tests with a fake search: chips for `ext:` and `size:`; an invalid token shows its message; Enter opens at the line; a new key cancels the previous preview.

#### G12 — Collections in the store

- **Change:** migration: `collections (id, name, kind TEXT CHECK (kind IN ('search','snapshot','manual')), query TEXT, base TEXT, created_ms, updated_ms)` and `collection_items (collection_id, location TEXT, added_ms, PRIMARY KEY (collection_id, location))`. API: `list`, `create`, `rename`, `delete`, `add_items` (returns the ids added, for undo), `remove_items`, `items`. Names unique, 1–60 characters.
- **Check:** db tests: create and list; add twice keeps one row; delete cascades; migration from the current schema.

#### G13 — Keep and open collections

- **Change:** Ctrl+S in a results tab, or "Keep as collection" in Ctrl+K there, opens a small dialog: Name, then "Saved search: runs again when opened" or "Snapshot: keeps these N files". Collections mode in Ctrl+K lists them with kind and count; Enter opens one as a tab `collection:<id>`: a saved search reruns; a snapshot or manual collection lists its items, and missing files show greyed with "missing".
- **Check:** tests: keep as snapshot then open lists the files; a deleted file shows as missing; a saved search reruns.

#### G14 — Add to a collection by hand

- **Change:** "Add to collection" in Ctrl+K (pick or create a collection) and Alt+Insert (the last used one) add the cursor file or the marked files to a manual collection. The status line says "Added <name> to <collection>" with Ctrl+Z, which removes exactly those items. A collection tab shows a From column with each file's folder.
- **Check:** tests: Alt+Insert adds the marked files; Ctrl+Z removes them; the From column text.

#### G17a — Content engine trial (D88)

- **Change:** add `grep-searcher` =0.1.17, `grep-regex` =0.1.14 and `encoding_rs_io` =0.1.8 (or their newest stable releases, pinned) to `navo-core`. New `navo-core/src/find/grep.rs`: `scan_grep(reader, &Compiled, cancel) -> Result<FileHits, io::Error>` with G2's contract: BOM sniffing through `encoding_rs_io`, Windows-1252 for lines that are not UTF-8, binary skip on a NUL in the first 8 KiB (`BinaryDetection::quit`), at most 1,000 hits per file, hit text cut to 200 characters. The G2 tests run against both `scan` and `scan_grep`. A `bench-walk` target `find-content` runs both over one generated 100,000-file tree and prints files per second and peak memory.
- **Check:** `cargo nextest run -p navo-core find::tests::matcher` passes for both engines; `cargo xtask check -p xtask`; `cargo deny check`.
- **Owner check:** `xtask.exe bench-walk <tree> --targets find-content --runs 5` on the Windows laptop.
- **Stop if:** a G2 test fails for `scan_grep` and the fix needs a test change; or the crates need `unsafe` in our code.

#### G17b — Adopt the faster content engine

- **Change:** if the owner's numbers show `scan_grep` at least as fast as `scan`, `scan` calls the grep engine and the old body goes. Otherwise the three crates and `grep.rs` go, and the numbers go in the Log. The bench target stays.
- **Check:** `cargo nextest run -p navo-core find`; `cargo nextest run -p navo-fs find`.

#### G18a — Ranking benchmark (D88)

- **Change:** a `bench-walk` target `rank`: 1,000,000 generated path names (the `index` target's generator) and 10 fixed queries, scored by today's `navo-index::fuzzy::Fuzzy` (`nucleo-matcher` =0.3.1) and by `frizbee` =0.13.0 (or its newest stable release, pinned, in `xtask` only). It prints time per query, the match count and the top 10 of each engine for every query.
- **Check:** `cargo xtask check -p xtask -p navo-index`; `cargo deny check`.
- **Owner check:** the run on the Windows laptop. `frizbee`'s own numbers come from an AVX-512 machine, so only the laptop's numbers count.
- **Stop if:** `frizbee` does not build on MSVC or needs a nightly toolchain.

#### G18b — Adopt `frizbee` (conditional)

- **Change:** only if G18a shows `frizbee` at least twice as fast with top results the owner accepts. `Fuzzy` keeps its API and uses `frizbee`; `nucleo-matcher` stays as the fallback for inputs `frizbee` refuses. Otherwise `frizbee` leaves `xtask` and the numbers go in the Log. The high-level `nucleo` crate is never a hard dependency without a fallback.
- **Check:** `cargo nextest run -p navo-index fuzzy`; the existing tests pass unchanged.

## B8 · Look inside

D18 (owner pick D82, direction A of `.crew/design/preview-props/index.html`: the inspector column). This section covers the inspector, properties and thumbnails. Archives as folders stay in B8 (v1.0) and are atomised after I15, with their open question: which packing formats (zip only, or 7z too)? Safe extraction (no paths outside the target) is fixed. Libraries (D88): `zip` (take 9.1.3 or newer for its security fixes; today 9.0.3), `sevenz-rust2` and `tar`, which Navo already has; `zstd` and `liblzma` only as a measured trial for writing `.tar.zst` and `.tar.xz` (`ruzstd` and `lzma-rust2` keep reading). No RAR: a `.rar` never opens as a folder; Enter or F3 on one offers "Open with the default app".

What exists (checked at `c016ee35`): the preview is a side sheet over the passive pane (`navo-ui/src/window_view/preview/sheet.rs:73-100`, frame from the split ratio in `preview/frame.rs:7-27`); it follows the cursor and loads in the shared Preview pool (`preview/mod.rs:62-212`). `FsEntry` has `size`, `modified_ms` and `Attributes { hidden, system, readonly, link_to_folder }` only (`navo-fs/src/provider/mod.rs:23-37`): no created or accessed time, no archive bit, no owner. P43 added size on disk for size listings (`SizeEntry.on_disk`, `provider/mod.rs:43-48`). `JobKind::Hash` exists with no user (`navo-fs/src/io_queue/pools.rs:6-44`). Image decoding and PDF page shots exist in the viewers (`window_view/viewer/image/decode.rs`, `viewer/pdf/shots.rs`). `navo-shell` wraps Windows shell calls (`crates/navo-shell/src/`). In the file list, Ctrl+Q, Ctrl+I, Alt+Enter and Ctrl+Shift+T are free; Ctrl+B and Ctrl+I are viewer drawer keys (`commands/keymap.toml:1128-1134`).

#### I1 — File details

- **Change:** `Provider::details(&Location) -> Result<FileDetails, FsError>` with a default built from `metadata`. `FileDetails { kind, size, on_disk: Option<u64>, created_ms, modified_ms, accessed_ms: Option<i64>, attributes: DetailAttributes { readonly, hidden, system, archive }, owner: None, acl: Vec::new(), inherited_from: None }`. Local Windows: `GetFileAttributesExW` plus allocation size as P43 reads it; Linux: `statx` birth time when present, `blocks × 512` on disk, archive false.
- **Check:** tests on a temp file: times within 2 s of now; read-only flag after setting it with std; on-disk ≥ size rounded to a block.

#### I2 — Owner and permissions

- **Change:** Windows: `GetNamedSecurityInfoW` (owner and DACL), `LookupAccountSidW` for names ("BENCH\admin"), each allow ACE summarised as Full control, Modify, Read and run, Read, Write or Special; `inherited_from` names the nearest parent when the ACE is inherited. Linux: owner and group names and the mode as `rwx` rows. Read only; errors leave the fields empty with a note.
- **Check:** Windows CI test: the temp file's owner is the current user; Linux test: mode rows for 0644.

#### I3 — Set times and attributes

- **Change:** `ProviderOps::set_times(&Location, TimesChange { created, modified, accessed: Option<i64> })` and `set_attributes(&Location, AttrChange { readonly, hidden, system, archive: Option<bool> })`; `Capabilities.edit_properties` true for local volumes only. Windows: `SetFileTime` (opened with `FILE_WRITE_ATTRIBUTES` only) and `SetFileAttributesW`; Linux: `utimensat` for modified and accessed, read-only through the mode; others refuse with "This drive cannot change that."
- **Check:** tests: modified time set and read back; read-only on and off; a `None` field changes nothing.

#### I4 — Properties job

- **Change:** `JobKind::SetProperties { targets, times, attrs }` in the job engine, under the mutation lease. Before each change it records the old values in the journal; failures per file are collected, the rest continue. An undo step restores the recorded values. Folders are changed themselves, never recursively.
- **Check:** job tests: 3 files changed; one read-only failure listed, two done; undo restores all old times and attributes; cancel after the first file leaves a correct journal.

#### I5 — Hashes on demand

- **Change:** new `navo-fs/src/hash.rs`: `hash_file(path, Algo { Crc32, Md5, Sha256 }, cancel, progress) -> Result<String, FsError>`, lower-case hex, 1 MiB reads, run in the `JobKind::Hash` pool. Dependencies: `md-5` and `sha2` from RustCrypto, pinned; `crc32fast` as today.
- **Check:** tests against known vectors for the empty file and "abc"; cancel returns `Cancelled`.

#### I6 — Inspector settings

- **Change:** `PreviewViewer` gains `inspector: bool` (default false), `inspector-width: u16` (360, clamped 280–560) and `inspector-properties: bool` (true). Update the default settings snapshot.
- **Check:** settings tests; the snapshot diff shows only the three keys.

#### I7 — Inspector column

- **Change:** new `window_view/inspector.rs`. Ctrl+Q in the file list toggles `preview.inspector`. At window widths of 1200 px and more (Q15) the body becomes tiles plus a column of `inspector-width` on the right with a drag handle; the column has a "Preview" header with Ctrl+Q and the current preview body (the existing loaders, `preview_body`), then a "Properties" header with Ctrl+I and Alt+Enter, and an empty properties area until I8. Below 1200 px, Ctrl+Q opens the existing preview side sheet instead. The column follows the active pane's cursor as the sheet does; F3 on a folder focuses the column. Ctrl+I folds the properties part.
- **Check:** tests: Ctrl+Q at 1600 px shows the column and saves the setting; at 1000 px it opens the side sheet; dragging the handle saves the width; the cursor move reloads the preview.
- **Stop if:** the preview body cannot render outside `SideSheet` without changing `preview/sheet.rs` beyond its frame; report.

#### I8 — Properties view

- **Change:** new `window_view/inspector/properties.rs` and `inspector/mixed.rs`. Sections per the prototype: General (name or "N files", type, location, size and on disk with exact bytes, a folder's contents); Dates (created, modified, accessed); Attributes (four check boxes, read only here); Owner and permissions (owner, inherited from, ACL rows); Hashes (CRC32, MD5, SHA-256 rows with Compute; while running a progress bar; then the value and Copy; moving the cursor cancels). For marked files, `mixed.rs` folds values: equal values show once, different ones show "Mixed · N values" and the range; attributes show "(2 of 3)". Details load off the UI thread through `details`.
- **Check:** tests with a fake provider: one file shows its fields; three marked files show a mixed date and "(2 of 3)"; Compute shows the hash and Copy puts it on the clipboard.

#### I9 — Edit properties

- **Change:** dates become text fields (typed in the user's date format; Ctrl+T inside a date field sets now), attribute boxes become tri-state for marked files (a mixed box stays untouched unless clicked). Changed fields are marked; the footer shows "N changes · Ctrl+Enter applies", Revert and Apply (or "Apply to N"). Alt+Enter moves focus into the properties; Esc returns to the list. Apply submits the I4 job; when it ends, the status line offers Ctrl+Z, which runs the job's undo. Non-local drives show the fields read only (`edit_properties` false). Editable scope per Q16.
- **Check:** tests: change modified time and Apply submits one job with that change; Revert clears; a mixed box untouched sends `None`; Ctrl+Z after the job calls undo.

#### I10 — Thumbnail service

- **Change:** new `navo-ui/src/thumbs/` (`mod.rs`, `cache.rs`). `Thumbs::request(location, stamp: (size, modified_ms), edge: u16) -> Option<Arc<RenderImage>>` returns a cached thumb or queues one in a `JobKind::Preview` queue and notifies when ready. Sources behind a `ThumbSource` trait: images with the image viewer's decoder, PDF first pages with the PDF page shots; others return none (I11b adds the shell). Scaling uses `fast_image_resize` =6.1.0 (or its newest stable release, pinned); the check times it against the `image` crate's resize on 500 photos, and it stays only if it is faster (D88). LRU cache by (location, stamp, edge), 64 MB budget, registered in `CacheRegistry` as "Thumbnails". Requests for rows that scroll out are cancelled.
- **Check:** tests: a PNG gives a thumb no larger than the edge; a second request hits the cache; a changed stamp reloads; the cache stays under budget with 500 images. The resize timing for both scalers is in the report.

#### I11a — Thumbnail helper process

- **Change:** `navo.exe --thumb-helper` starts a child process under a Job Object, as `--pdf-helper` does. A pipe protocol carries `(path, edge)` in and RGBA bytes or an error out. The client restarts a dead helper, gives each request 2 s, and is a `ThumbSource` in `navo-app`. The helper starts on the first request, not at start-up, and exits after 60 s idle.
- **Check:** tests with a fake helper: a reply arrives; a crashed helper restarts once and the request fails cleanly; a hung request times out; the helper exits when idle.
- **Stop if:** the PDFium helper code cannot be shared without changing `navo-pdf`'s public API; report.

#### I11b — Shell thumbnails on Windows

- **Change:** `navo-shell/src/thumbs.rs`: `IShellItemImageFactory::GetImage` with `SIIGBF_THUMBNAILONLY` at the requested edge, converted to RGBA. Only the I11a helper calls it; the main process never loads shell thumbnail handlers. Tried after Navo's own decoders.
- **Check:** a Windows CI test gets a thumbnail for a generated `.bmp` through the helper; Linux builds without it.

#### I12 — Thumbnail rows

- **Change:** Ctrl+Shift+T toggles thumbnail rows for the active pane (saved per pane in the session like other view parts). Rows grow to 48 px; the icon column becomes 40 px and shows the thumb when ready, the icon until then. Only visible rows request thumbs. Columns still line up (L1) and drop (L2).
- **Check:** tests: the toggle changes row height; a ready thumb replaces the icon; L0 alignment tests pass with thumbnail rows on.

#### I13 — Marked files in the inspector

- **Change:** with two or more marked files, the inspector's preview part shows "N files marked · size" and a contact sheet of thumbnails (or icons) with names, at most 24 then "+N".
- **Check:** tests: three marked files show three figures; thirty show 24 and "+6".

## B9 · Always at hand

D87 (owner, R12 direction 1 of `design/prototypes/clipboard/index.html`): clipboard history and saved items in a Ctrl+K-style palette, built as a module you can switch off. The tray and the Navo summon key are done in the speed phase (SP10a–SP10d on `main`: `[tray]` in `navo.toml`, `keep-running` off by default per SQ1, `summon-key` Win+Alt+N per SQ2). B9 ships in two pre-releases: **B9a** history (CB1–CB21), then **B9b** saved items, secrets and notes (CB22–CB31). All atoms are `blocked` until the orchestrator confirms them and Q21–Q26 are answered or their defaults accepted.

What exists (checked at `d4700f1c`, paths under `crates/`): the resident thread owns one global hotkey (`HOTKEY_ID = 1`) and the tray on a null-window message loop (`navo-app/src/resident/thread.rs:112-169`, `hotkey.rs:1-30`, `keys.rs:1-46`); events become `OpenRequest { summoned_at, quit, … }` (`resident/host.rs:64-75`, `navo-ui/src/window_view/hand_off.rs:46-90`); `ResidentHooks::apply` restarts the thread when settings change (`host.rs:36-60`). The summon key parser is `navo-ui/src/settings/summon_key.rs`; the Tray settings rows are `settings/tray_fields.rs` and `Section::Tray` (`settings/fields.rs:8-90`). The Explorer file clipboard is `navo-shell/src/clipboard/` (CF_HDROP read and write, "Preferred DropEffect", `Busy` error) behind `FileClipboardService` (`navo-app/src/clipboard.rs`, `navo-ui/src/window_view/clipboard.rs:1-60`); text copies go through GPUI `cx.write_to_clipboard` (for example `window_view/shell_menu/actions.rs:68`). Nothing watches the clipboard. Side layers follow the Recently deleted pattern: `RetainedHooks` (`navo-ui/src/browser/retained.rs:33-54`), `window_view/recent_sheet/`, the command group `commands/retained.rs` and the layer list in `window_view/layout.rs:34-60`. `navo.db` opens in `navo-store/src/db/mod.rs:90-210` (WAL, `synchronous = NORMAL`). Ctrl+Shift+V is bound only in multi-rename (`commands/keymap.toml:1074-1076`) and in the F4 editor (`keymap.toml:1338-1340`). GPUI has `WindowKind::PopUp` (vendored in `vendor/gpui-pre-windows/src/window.rs`); window options are built in `navo-ui/src/title_bar/mod.rs:28-40`. The F4 editor uses gpui-kit `EditorState` for multi-line text (`window_view/viewer/editor/mod.rs:12-40`). Near the 400-line limit: `window_view/mod.rs` 384, `settings/values.rs` 388, `command_center/view.rs` 388.

### Decisions for B9

D21 is D87 applied to this plan. A worker that finds one wrong stops and reports.

- D21a — **Module boundary.** A new crate `navo-clipboard` holds the model, the admit rules, the store, search and the service. Only `navo-app` depends on it. `navo-ui` sees a `ClipboardHooks` trait with its own row types, as `RetainedHooks` does. OS calls live in `navo-shell` as general capabilities (watch, private write, DPAPI, paste back). With `clipboard.enabled = false` the app opens no store, starts no watcher, registers no Win+Alt+V, and the commands are disabled and hidden from Ctrl+K; the data stays on disk. B12 later puts the same pieces behind the module registry without changing `navo-ui` callers.
- D21b — **Own database file (approved by the orchestrator 2026-10-30; differs from D17).** D17 puts user data in `navo.db`. B9 uses `<data>/clipboard.db` instead (Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Navo\clipboard.db`; portable mode: the profile's `data` folder), with its own `user_version` migrations, WAL, `synchronous = NORMAL` and `secure_delete = ON`. Reasons: `navo.db` holds the operations journal; a module that can be switched off and wiped should not share its file, migrations or salvage; "Delete all clipboard data" can remove one file set. Cost: a second SQLite file and connection. Approved: CB2 may start.
- D21c — **What history keeps.** Text (`CF_UNICODETEXT`) and file lists (`CF_HDROP` paths plus copy or cut; paths only, never file contents), the source app (the clipboard owner's exe name, best effort) and the time. Never recorded: copies that carry `ExcludeClipboardContentFromMonitorProcessing` or `Clipboard Viewer Ignore`, or `CanIncludeInClipboardHistory` = 0 (password managers set these; the R12 prototype promises it); Navo's own secret copies (they carry the same formats); empty or whitespace-only text; images and other formats; anything over the caps (Q23). The same text or path list again moves the existing entry to the top (key: SHA-256 of kind and body, `sha2` =0.10.9 as in `navo-fs`). The 500 newest stay (D87); the trim runs in the insert's transaction.
- D21d — **Data safety.** *Never logged:* no clipboard text, path list, title, note, saved body or secret reaches tracing, the diagnostic event ring, crash records or the diagnostic report; logs carry kind, length, count and error kind only; CB19 proves it with a canary. *Clear:* "Clear clipboard history" deletes history rows only (saved items, secrets and notes stay) and then runs `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`; "Delete all clipboard data" closes the store, removes `clipboard.db`, `-wal` and `-shm`, and starts empty; both ask first and name the counts. *Corrupt:* open runs `quick_check`; a corrupt or unreadable file is renamed `clipboard.db.corrupt-<ms>` with its `-wal` and `-shm`, never deleted; Navo starts with an empty store and the palette shows one line about it. *One writer:* the module's worker thread; the profile lock already allows one Navo per profile. History text is stored unencrypted in the user's profile (Q24); secrets are sealed (D21f). *Exception (owner, 2026-11-02):* an explicit "delete all clipboard data" by the user also removes the `.corrupt-*` copies.
- D21e — **Threads (SD2).** The `navo-shell` watcher thread reads the clipboard (message-only window, `AddClipboardFormatListener`); a `Busy` clipboard is retried 5 times 20 ms apart, then that change is dropped with a debug line that names no content. Store writes run on the module worker. The UI never touches the OS clipboard or the store for history; it reads an `Arc` snapshot with a revision and drops stale replies.
- D21f — **Secrets (D87).** The body is sealed with DPAPI (`CryptProtectData`, current user, `CRYPTPROTECT_UI_FORBIDDEN`); title and user name stay plain and searchable; no snapshot holds plain text; `reveal` returns a wrapper that zeroes its buffer on drop. Copying writes the private formats of D21c and keeps the clipboard sequence number; after 30 s, or at quit if sooner, Navo empties the clipboard only when the sequence is unchanged. Linux builds show "Secrets need Windows" in Secrets mode and save no secret.
- D21g — **Keys (D87, SQ2).** Ctrl+Shift+V in the global context opens the palette in the window; the editor and multi-rename keep their own Ctrl+Shift+V. There Enter copies, Enter on a path goes to it, Ctrl+V on a file list pastes it into the active pane. Win+Alt+V (`clipboard.summon-key`; changeable; empty turns it off; a taken key shows the Tray section's notice) opens the palette in its own pop-up window over the current app while Navo runs; there Enter pastes into that app and Ctrl+C only copies. The pop-up is made on the first summon and hidden, not closed, afterwards. Win+Alt+N stays the Navo summon. Palette keys follow the prototype (`index.html:736-760`): Tab and Shift+Tab modes, Up and Down, Enter, Ctrl+C, Ctrl+S save from history, Ctrl+N new, F2 edit (on a row) or Manage, Del delete, Alt+R reveal, Alt+U copy user, Esc clears the search, then closes.
- D21h — **Look: compact direction 2 (owner pick, 2026-11-02; `design/prototypes/clipboard-compact/index.html`, `?v=2`).** A 720 × 400 palette: list 264 px wide with 28 px rows and a preview pane on the right; text-only mode tabs with counts; no action strip, actions only in a Ctrl+K menu; a 28 px footer showing the Enter label and Actions; below 560 px wide the preview docks under the list. The folder note stays on top of Notes; F2 "Manage…" keeps the two-column library of the first prototype (`design/prototypes/clipboard/index.html:573-655`), which the compact round did not redraw. Behaviour and keys are unchanged (D21g). Code (`index.html` line ranges): palette `p2` `:504-518`, preview pane `:497-503`, rows `:399-427`, actions menu `:428-448`, empty states `:449-458`, footer `:460-468`, toast `:259-265,597-607`, styles `:75-108,154-258`. Command-center row parts and theme tokens only; fade only, about 80 ms, none with reduced motion; icons centred on the first text line (D86). Modes show only what the hooks offer: B9a shows All and Recent; B9b adds Saved, Secrets and Notes.

### Data kept on disk

| Data | Where | Cap | Cleared by | Never |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| History (text, path lists, source app, times) | `clipboard.db` table `clips` | 500 entries (D87); size per Q23 | Del on a row; Clear clipboard history; Delete all clipboard data | logged; recorded when private (D21c); stored in plain text, see Q24 |
| Saved items and notes | `clipboard.db` tables `saved`, `notes` | size per entry per Q23 | Del, Delete all clipboard data | logged; trimmed by the history cap |
| Secrets | `clipboard.db` `saved.sealed` (DPAPI blob) | as saved items | Del, Delete all clipboard data | plain on disk, in a snapshot, in history or in logs |
| Settings | `navo.toml` `[clipboard]` | — | Settings reset | — |

Order: CB1, CB6, CB8, CB13 and CB23 start at once (new crate, `navo-shell`, `navo-ui` contract; no shared files; the `navo-shell` atoms run one at a time on the VM). CB1 → CB2 → CB3; CB1 → CB4; CB2 + CB4 → CB5. CB6 → CB7. Q21 → CB9 → CB10 → CB11 (also after CB8) → CB12. CB13 → CB15 → CB16, CB17. CB3 + CB5 + CB7 + CB9 + CB13 → CB14 → CB19. CB12 + CB14 + CB16 → CB18. B9a ships after CB20 and CB21. B9b: CB3 → CB22 → CB24 (also after CB5); CB14 + CB23 + CB24 → CB25 → CB26 (also after Q26), CB27 → CB28; CB25 + CB16 → CB29.

#### CB1 — Model and admit rules

- **Change:** new crate `crates/navo-clipboard` (`Cargo.toml` with `sha2` =0.10.9 and `thiserror`; `src/lib.rs`, `src/model.rs`, `src/admit.rs`). No dependency on `navo-core`, `navo-ui` or `navo-shell`. `Captured`, `Entry { content, source_app: Option<String>, first_ms, last_ms, uses: u32 }`, `Saved { id, kind, title, body: Option<String>, user: Option<String>, scope, created_ms, updated_ms }`, `Limits { max_text_bytes, max_paths, max_entries }` with the Q23 defaults. `admit` trims nothing: it keeps or skips with a `Reason { Empty, TooLarge, TooManyPaths }`. `content_key` is SHA-256 over a kind byte and the UTF-8 text or the paths joined by NUL plus the cut flag.
- **Check:** tests: whitespace-only text skipped; text one byte over the cap skipped, at the cap kept; the same paths with copy and with cut give different keys; equal text gives equal keys.

#### CB2 — History store

- **Change:** `src/store/mod.rs` and `src/store/history.rs`. `ClipStore::open(path)` creates the file, sets WAL, `synchronous = NORMAL`, `secure_delete = ON`, `foreign_keys = ON`, and migrates by `user_version` (V1: `clips (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, key BLOB UNIQUE, kind INTEGER, body TEXT, paths TEXT, cut INTEGER, source_app TEXT, first_ms, last_ms, uses)`). A newer `user_version` refuses with `ClipError::TooNew`. `record(entry)` in one transaction: an existing key moves to the top (`last_ms`, `uses + 1`), else inserts; then deletes the oldest rows beyond `max_entries`. `history(limit)` newest first; `delete(id)`; `clear_history()` deletes all rows, then `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`.
- **Data:** D21b, D21d. Nothing here logs a body.
- **Check:** temp-dir tests: 501 records keep 500 and drop the oldest; a repeat moves to the top and counts uses; a reopened store lists the same rows; a V99 file refuses.

#### CB3 — Store safety

- **Change:** `ClipStore::open` runs `PRAGMA quick_check`; on failure, or when SQLite reports `NotADatabase` or `DatabaseCorrupt`, it renames `clipboard.db`, `-wal` and `-shm` to `clipboard.db.corrupt-<ms>` (and the same suffix for the other two), opens a fresh file and returns `Opened::MovedAside(PathBuf)`. It never deletes a file it did not create in that call. `wipe_all(self, path)` closes the connection, removes the three files and reopens empty. Errors keep the old files.
- **Check:** tests: a file of random bytes is moved aside and a fresh store opens; `wipe_all` leaves no `-wal` or `-shm`; after `clear_history` and after `wipe_all`, a canary string is absent from the bytes of every file in the folder.
- **Stop if:** Windows cannot rename the files while another handle is open in the test; report.

#### CB4 — Search

- **Change:** `src/search.rs`. `Mode { All, Recent, Saved, Secrets, Notes }` with the prototype's filters: Saved excludes secrets and notes. Case-insensitive substring over title and body; for path lists over the joined paths; secrets match title and user name only. `Found` carries byte ranges for highlights. All lists history first, then saved items. Notes mode puts notes whose scope equals the given folder (case-insensitive on Windows-style paths) first.
- **Check:** tests per mode; a secret's body never matches; a folder note sorts first; an empty query returns all in order.

#### CB5 — Service

- **Change:** `src/service/mod.rs` and `src/service/worker.rs`. `ClipService::start(store, limits, sink: Arc<dyn ClipSink>) -> ClipService` owns one thread and a bounded channel (64). Inputs: `captured(Captured)`, `copy(id, How { Copy, Paste })`, `delete(id)`, `clear_history()`, `wipe_all()`, `set_recording(bool)`. Out: `snapshot() -> Arc<Snapshot>` with a `revision: u64` and a `subscribe(Box<dyn Fn(u64) + Send>)` callback. `ClipSink { write_text(&str), write_files(&[PathBuf], cut: bool) }` is the port; the worker calls it, never the UI. While recording is off, captured events are dropped. `stop()` drains the channel and joins with a 2 s limit, as `resident/thread.rs` does.
- **Check:** tests with a fake sink and a temp store: a captured text appears in the next snapshot with a higher revision; a repeat does not add a row; recording off drops captures; `copy` calls the sink once; `stop` returns within 2 s with work queued.

#### CB6 — Clipboard watcher (Windows)

- **Change:** `navo-shell/src/clipboard/watch.rs` (Windows) and a Unix stub that returns `ClipboardError::Unsupported`. `Watcher::start(on_change: Box<dyn Fn(ShellCapture) + Send>) -> Result<Watcher, ClipboardError>` makes a thread with a message-only window (`HWND_MESSAGE`) and `AddClipboardFormatListener`. On `WM_CLIPBOARDUPDATE` it opens the clipboard (retry `Busy` 5 × 20 ms), skips when `ExcludeClipboardContentFromMonitorProcessing` or `Clipboard Viewer Ignore` is present or `CanIncludeInClipboardHistory` holds a DWORD 0, then reads `CF_UNICODETEXT`, else `CF_HDROP` through the existing reader. `ShellCapture { text | files, cut, owner_exe: Option<String>, sequence }`; the owner exe comes from `GetClipboardOwner` → process image name, file stem only. `Drop` removes the listener, destroys the window and joins.
- **Check:** Windows tests: text set by a helper arrives once; text set with `CanIncludeInClipboardHistory` = 0 does not; a file list arrives with `cut`; drop stops the thread. Linux: the stub builds.
- **Stop if:** `CF_HDROP` reading needs changes to `windows.rs` beyond making a reader `pub(crate)`; report.

#### CB7 — Clipboard text writes (Windows)

- **Change:** `navo-shell/src/clipboard/text.rs`, callable from any thread (the module worker, never the UI thread): `write_text` sets `CF_UNICODETEXT` with the `Busy` retry of CB6; `write_private_text` sets `CF_UNICODETEXT` and the three formats `ExcludeClipboardContentFromMonitorProcessing`, `CanIncludeInClipboardHistory` = 0, `CanUploadToCloudClipboard` = 0, and returns `GetClipboardSequenceNumber` after closing; `clear_if_sequence(seq)` empties the clipboard only when the number is unchanged. Unix: all three return `Unsupported`; the palette then says "Copying from the palette works on Windows only in the beta."
- **Check:** Windows tests: the CB6 watcher records a `write_text` once and ignores a private write; `clear_if_sequence` clears an unchanged clipboard and leaves a newer copy alone.

#### CB8 — Paste back (Windows)

- **Change:** `navo-shell/src/paste_back.rs`. `Foreground::now()` keeps `GetForegroundWindow` and its thread id; `Foreground::paste(&self) -> Result<(), PasteError>` checks `IsWindow`, calls `SetForegroundWindow`, waits until it is foreground (at most 200 ms), then sends Ctrl+V with `SendInput`. Errors: `Gone`, `Refused` (focus not given). Unix stub: `Unsupported`.
- **Check:** Windows test: a test window takes focus back and receives Ctrl+V; a destroyed window gives `Gone`.

- **Check:** settings tests; the snapshot diff shows only the new table.

Rows: "Clipboard and saved items" (toggle), "Record clipboard history" (toggle, Windows only), "Open from anywhere" (text, the summon-key parser and messages, Windows only). Help text names Q22's rule. Accessors go in a new `settings/clipboard_values.rs` because `values.rs` is at 388 lines.
- **Check:** settings tests: defaults; the two Windows-only rows hidden on Linux; an invalid key shows the parser's message.

#### CB11 — Second hotkey

- **Change:** `CLIPBOARD_ID = 2` in `resident/keys.rs`; `ResidentConfig` gains `clipboard_key: Option<SummonKey>` from `clipboard.summon-key`, only when `clipboard.enabled`; `is_idle` counts it. `thread.rs` registers it like the summon key and keeps a separate taken flag. On `WM_HOTKEY` with `CLIPBOARD_ID` it takes `Foreground::now()` (CB8) before anything else and sends `ResidentEvent::Clipboard { at, previous }`.
- **Check:** tests: `event_for(Input::Hotkey(2))` gives the clipboard event; a config with only the clipboard key is not idle; disabled module gives no clipboard key.

#### CB12 — Clipboard summon request

- **Change:** `OpenRequest` gains `clipboard: Option<ClipboardSummon { at, previous: Option<Arc<dyn PasteTarget>> }>` where `PasteTarget` is a small trait in `hand_off.rs` (`fn paste(&self) -> Result<(), String>`), so `navo-ui` never sees `navo-shell` types. `request_for` maps the event; `ResidentHooks` gains `clipboard_key_taken()`. `hand_off.rs` only stores the request for CB18; no window change yet.

#### CB13 — Hooks contract

- **Change:** new `navo-ui/src/clip_palette/mod.rs` (module root, hooks only for now) and `clip_palette/hooks.rs`: `ClipboardHooks: Send + Sync` with `modes() -> Vec<ClipMode>`, `search(&str, ClipMode, folder: Option<&Location>) -> ClipResults { rows: Vec<ClipRow>, kept: u32, revision: u64, notice: Option<String> }`, `copy(ClipId, How)`, `delete(ClipId)`, `clear_history()`, `delete_all()`, `set_recording(bool)`, `subscribe(Box<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>)`, and for B9b `save_from_history`, `save(SavedDraft)`, `note_for(&Location)`, `set_note(Scope, String)`, `reveal(ClipId) -> Result<SecretText, String>`, `copy_user(ClipId)`. `ClipRow { id, kind, title, detail, source_app, when_ms, scope, from: Origin, ranges }`. `NoClipboard` returns no modes and refuses with `clipboard-off`. `StartOptions.clipboard: Option<Arc<dyn ClipboardHooks>>`; if `window_view/mod.rs` passes 400 lines, move `StartOptions` to `window_view/start_options.rs` first in the same atom.

When `clipboard.enabled`: open `ClipStore` at `dirs.data.join("clipboard.db")`, start `ClipService` with a sink over GPUI-free `navo-shell` writes (text: CB7's `write_text`, files: `write_files`), start the `navo-shell` watcher and map `ShellCapture` to `Captured`; implement `ClipboardHooks` over the service (B9b methods refuse until CB25). `apply(values)` starts or stops it live; stopping leaves the files. `Opened::MovedAside` becomes the hooks' `notice`. One field in `gui.rs`.

Esc clears the search, then closes. Results refresh on `subscribe` and drop replies with an older revision.

Enter: in the window copies (path text: "Go to" navigates the active pane); Ctrl+C copies only; Ctrl+V on a file list puts it back on the file clipboard and runs the existing paste into the active pane; Del deletes the row. Each action closes or stays as the prototype does and shows a status line ("Copied", "Deleted"). The from-anywhere variant is CB18.

Clear and Delete all confirm with counts through `confirm_dialog`. The palette layer joins `window_view/layout.rs`. Strings in `navo-l10n/locales/en-US`.

The main window stays as it was (hidden stays hidden). One pop-up per app.

- **Change:** migration V2: `saved (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, kind INTEGER, title TEXT NOT NULL, body TEXT, user TEXT, sealed BLOB, scope TEXT, created_ms, updated_ms)` and `notes (scope TEXT PRIMARY KEY COLLATE NOCASE, body TEXT, updated_ms)` where scope `''` is the global note list key per Q26. API: `saved()`, `save(Saved)`, `update`, `delete_saved`, `note(scope)`, `set_note(scope, body)` (empty body deletes the row). Secrets keep `body` NULL and `sealed` set; the store never sees plain secret text.

- **Check:** tests: V1 file migrates and keeps history; a note set twice keeps one row; a secret row without `sealed` is refused.

#### CB23 — DPAPI seal (Windows)

- **Change:** `navo-shell/src/dpapi.rs`: `seal(&[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, SealError>` and `unseal(&[u8]) -> Result<SecretBytes, SealError>` over `CryptProtectData` and `CryptUnprotectData` (current user, `CRYPTPROTECT_UI_FORBIDDEN`), freeing the output with `LocalFree`; `SecretBytes` zeroes its buffer on drop. Adds the `Win32_Security_Cryptography` feature. Unix: `SealError::Unsupported`.
- **Check:** Windows tests: round trip; a flipped byte fails to unseal; the sealed bytes do not contain the input.

#### CB24 — Service: saved items, notes, secrets

- **Change:** the service gains saved CRUD, `save_from_history(id)`, notes, and secrets through a `Sealer` port (`seal`, `unseal`) and two new sink calls (`write_private`, `clear_if_sequence`). Copying a secret starts a 30 s timer; at expiry, or at `stop()` if sooner, it calls `clear_if_sequence`. `reveal(id)` unseals on the worker and returns `SecretBytes`. Snapshots carry secret titles and users only.
- **Check:** tests with a fake sealer, sink and clock: a secret copy clears after 30 s; a newer copy is not cleared; `stop` before 30 s clears; no snapshot field contains the secret; the fake sealer's input never reaches the store unsealed.

- **Change:** `clip_module/` wires CB7's private write and CB23 into the CB24 ports, implements the B9b hook methods, and adds Saved, Secrets and Notes to `modes()`. On Linux the Secrets mode shows "Secrets need Windows" and `save` refuses the secret kind.

Ctrl+E focuses the editor, Esc leaves it. From anywhere, Notes lists all notes. Behaviour for renamed or missing folders per Q26.

Ctrl+N and F2 on a saved row open a form: kind, title (required, 1–80 characters), body (`EditorState`), scope (Global or this folder), and for secrets user name and a masked password with Alt+R. Ctrl+Enter or Ctrl+S saves, Esc cancels after asking when changed.

- **Check:** tests: Alt+R reveals then moving hides; the countdown text; Ctrl+S creates a saved row.

### Open questions for B9

Already decided, not asked again: 500 entries kept across restarts (D87); secrets and copies apps mark private are never recorded (D87, R12 prototype); Win+Alt+V from anywhere and Ctrl+Shift+V inside Navo (D87); Win+Alt+N for Navo (SQ2); tray off by default (SQ1); notes per folder or global (D87); Enter pastes into the previous app from anywhere (D87); secrets cleared after 30 s (D87).

21. **Q21 — Module and recording on by default?** *Recommended: yes to both; the first palette open shows one line: "Navo keeps what you copy on this PC. Settings › Clipboard turns it off."* **Answered 2026-10-30 (owner): as recommended.**
22. **Q22 — Recording when the window is closed.** *Recommended: history and Win+Alt+V work only while Navo runs. With "Keep Navo in the tray" off (SQ1), closing the window stops both; Settings › Clipboard says so. The module never turns the tray on by itself.* **Answered 2026-10-30 (owner): as recommended.**
23. **Q23 — Size and age caps.** *Recommended: 500 entries, no age limit; text up to 256 KiB per entry and 32 MiB of history text in total (oldest trimmed); file lists up to 10,000 paths; larger copies are skipped, never cut; images not recorded; saved items and notes up to 256 KiB each, no count limit.* **Answered 2026-10-30 (owner): as recommended.**
24. **Q24 — Privacy controls.** *Recommended: Pause/Resume history in Ctrl+K and Settings; Clear clipboard history and Delete all clipboard data, both confirmed; no per-app exclusion list in the beta; history text stored unencrypted in the user profile, as Windows' own Win+V history; only secrets use DPAPI.* **Answered 2026-10-30 (owner): as recommended.**
25. **Q25 — Ctrl+K and the B12 Commands module.** *Recommended: Ctrl+K gets the four clipboard commands only; saved items do not show in Ctrl+K results in the beta. B9's "command" kind is plain saved text with no `{VAR}` or `<name>` fill-ins; the B12 Commands module stays separate and reads `commands.json`.* **Answered 2026-10-30 (owner): as recommended.**
26. **Q26 — Folder notes after a rename or move.** *Recommended: a note stays with its path; when the folder is gone it lists under Other notes as "folder not found", and F2 can point it at another folder. Network folders may have notes.* **Answered 2026-10-30 (owner): as recommended.**

### Done when

- B9a: copying text or files anywhere on Windows adds one entry; a private copy adds none; Ctrl+Shift+V and Win+Alt+V open the palette; Enter copies inside Navo and pastes into the previous app from anywhere; Clear and Delete all leave no content in `clipboard.db` bytes; the canary test passes; turning the module off stops recording at once and keeps the data.
- B9b: saved items, secrets and folder notes survive a restart; a secret is never readable in `clipboard.db`, history or logs, and the clipboard is cleared 30 s after copying it or at quit.
- Both: a review verdict, the full gate once, VM CI green, screenshots at 390 px and desktop in both themes, docs updated.

## Later milestones

Each is atomised after the previous milestone's review, from the code at that time. The open questions are answered before atomising.

### B10 · Never waits

Run P43 as written (Parts J, S and B), since its Q1–Q3 are answered. It adds the job pools, folder sizes that cancel on leave, size on disk, hard links counted once and `cargo xtask bench-walk`. Questions: do the activity strip and the job card show queued jobs per pool? Does "measure shown folders" stay opt-in (P43 D7)? Which Windows API gives allocation size cheapest on a Windows PC's network shares?

### B11 · Undo anything

Every job step becomes reversible, compensable or final (journal and recovery exist). Undo offers only what it can do. A history list shows recent jobs with an Undo button each. Per-case confirmations: one switch per case (Recycle, permanent delete, overwrite, drop, large copy), each "ask / don't ask". Questions: how far back does history go (count or days)? Can a job be undone after a restart? What does undo of a copy do to files the user edited since? Does undo of a Recycle Bin delete restore from the Recycle Bin? Design round R4.

### B12 · Modules

The module registry (D13): one manifest per native module, runtime on/off without restart, safe mode that starts with every module off. The settings window is generated from each module's schema; today's hand-written `FieldSpec` tables move behind the same schema. First modules: terminal (built in B4), copy path, folder search, the external viewer and editor leftovers, notifications for long operations. Commands module (owner, 2026-10-29), separate from the B9 clipboard history: a library of saved commands in sections, Enter copies, `{VAR}` from a profile, `<name>` fill-ins asked on copy; `{MOD}` can follow the active pane's folder; commands also appear in Ctrl+K; reads the owner's existing `commands.json` command-sheet format (a shared file on a network drive or in git); the summon key opens it. GPUI UI; only the data format and fill-in logic come from the owner's Tauri tool. Later, in v2.0 (D88): user scripts on `mlua` (Luau), then sandboxed plugins on `wasmtime`; an integrated terminal pane on `alacritty_terminal`. The registry should not block them, but none is built here. Questions: what starts safe mode (`--safe-mode`, an automatic offer after two crashed starts, or both)? Is the manifest TOML in the crate or a Rust const? How do module keys appear in the keymap when a module is off? Which current features become modules and which stay core? Design round R6.

### B13 · Network and copy power

Refresh mode per network location: watch, poll every N s, or manual. Ignore list: names and globs hidden from lists, search and sizes. Parallel and Large files copy modes, copy options (attributes, timestamps, verify), profiles and rules by destination; copies keep `CopyFile2` for progress and cancel. Remote locations (v2.0, D88) belong here too: SFTP on `russh` + `russh-sftp`, S3 and WebDAV on `opendal`, `rclone` only as an optional helper for cloud drives. Date and size formats. Questions: does the ignore list also apply to copy? Where does a network location's refresh mode live: per share root or per folder? Are rules matched by drive letter, volume label, volume kind or path prefix? Does a rule apply silently or show its name in the copy dialog? Which date formats are offered? Decimal or binary size units by default?

### B14 · Make it yours

Custom colour themes in TOML with live reload and a contrast check (D16); icon themes as data packages (SVG by extension, name and folder; shell icons kept for `.exe`, `.lnk`, `.ico`). Every shortcut changeable: finish what the user keymap lacks (conflict view, per-context keys, reset per key). Questions: is the optional Material Icon Theme (MIT) in or out? Which shortcuts cannot be changed today? Design round R8.

### B15 · More in F3

Raw/Pretty formatting on open for JSON, XML and YAML (`navo-structured` already parses them; the viewer needs a toggle in the D58 View menu and a format-on-open setting). The automotive syntax colours (m4, OIL, DCM, Notepad++ languages) on D64's palette. P38 A5 and A6 (CSV and CAN trace column resize) as written. Spreadsheets in F3 (read-only first; scope per Q6): `calamine` reads, `rust_xlsxwriter` exports (D88). Questions: does Pretty rewrite only the view or also offer "Save formatted"? (architect: view only.) Is a 50 MB XML formatted on open or only on request?

### B16 · Beta release

The installer and updates on Velopack (D88 replaces D73's NSIS), next to the portable zip; signed through Azure Artifact Signing if Navo is eligible (individuals: US and Canada only; check first), else unsigned as D73 says. Velopack's uninstall hook runs `navo.exe --index-service remove` (elevated), so the "Navo Index" and legacy "Navo Index" services, their copies in Program Files and the index in ProgramData go away with the app (owner, 2026-10-27). A soak test (scripted browsing, copying and viewing for 8 hours with `dhat` and handle counts). Steadier Windows tests (D74). Then the exit criteria below. Questions: does the soak run nightly in CI or on a dedicated machine? Does the installer offer "start with Windows", the tray and the index service?

## Designer rounds

Each round gives 3–4 directions behind one picker with neutral labels, in both themes, at 390 px and desktop, under `design/prototypes/<name>/`. The owner picks before any UI atom of that feature runs.

| Round | For | When | Atoms that wait |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R1 | Recently deleted sheet (B1) | Day one, in parallel with B1 backend atoms | S9, S10 |

| R3 | Viewer chrome and pins (B3) | After Q7 | V10 |
| R5 | Workspace switcher (B4) | done: direction D, the tray (D82) | — |
| R7 | Tiled panes: split, move, resize, focus and copy target (B6) | done: direction D plus free layouts (D82) | — |
| R9 | Find files dialog, results pane and virtual collections (B7) | done: direction A, Ctrl+K Content mode (D82) | — |

| R11 | Properties drawer (B8) | done with R10 (D82) | — |

Round numbers keep their old meaning; R9–R12 are new with D81. No round for B2 (D80 fixes the behaviour), B5 (D15 fixes the behaviour; contrast themes use system colours), B10, B13, B15 or B16. The rich map changes in B3 follow the owner's own description and need no round.

## Open questions for the owner

Architect defaults apply until answered.

1. **Q1 — Safety net defaults.** Retain Shift+Del and overwrites for everyone, 3 days, 1 GiB per drive, stop keeping below 256 MiB free? *Recommended: yes to all four; changeable 1–7 days in Settings > Safety net.* **Answered 2026-10-28 (owner): yes, as recommended.**
2. **Q2 — Recently deleted shape.** A side sheet now (D4), or a pane you browse like a folder? *Recommended: the side sheet in B1; a pane view can come with virtual collections in B7.*
3. **Q3 — Narrow window.** At what width does the window switch to one pane, and how do you reach the other? *Recommended: below 900 px; Tab switches; F5/F6 still copy to the hidden pane and show its path.*
4. **Q4 — Order after B2.** *Answered by D81: the owner's priority items come first; background work moves to B10.*
5. **Q5 — Copy target with more than two panes.** *Recommended: the most recently focused other pane, marked with a dashed outline and an "F5/F6 → here" tag (tiling TD8, from the D82 prototype). At most 4 panes in the beta.* Tiling's own questions are QT1–QT4 in `plans/2026-10-27-tiling.md`.
6. **Q6 — Spreadsheets in the beta.** *Recommended: read-only `.xlsx`, `.xls` and `.ods` with sheet tabs and frozen headers through `calamine` (0.37 or newer, pinned); formulas show their cached values; "Save as xlsx" through `rust_xlsxwriter`; anything more after 1.0.* B15 moved to v2.0 (D85).
7. **Q7 — What are viewer "pins"?** The roadmap says "symbol list, bookmarks, a coloured scrollbar strip and pins"; bookmarks and scrollbar ticks exist. *Recommended: a pin keeps a line or symbol visible in a strip at the top of the viewer while you scroll; pins are saved per file like bookmarks.*
8. **Q8 — "The map opens on one module."** At open, or when you pick one? *Recommended: the map opens on all modules as today; Enter on a module fills the map with its files; Backspace returns; the last module is remembered per file.*
9. **Q9 — Workspaces per window or per app?** *Recommended: per window (D14); an empty slot starts as a copy of the current layout; Alt+1…9 work in the file list, not in the viewer, where Alt+1, Alt+3 and Alt+5 switch the view.*
10. **Q10 — A key for "Open terminal here"?** *Recommended: none by default; it is in Ctrl+K and the empty-space menu, and you can bind one.*
11. **Q11 — Do the items moved into the beta come before background work?** D81 marks them as moved, not as priority. *Recommended: yes, B7–B9 before B10, because the owner asked for them by name.*
12. **Q12 — Workspace changes: saved at once, or a draft?** The first D14 saved on every switch; the picked tray prototype keeps changes as a draft ("Draft · 2") until Ctrl+S, with Save and Discard on each card. *Answered 2026-10-27: the draft, as recommended.*
13. **Q13 — Alt+5 when only three workspaces exist.** The first D14 made an empty slot a copy of the current layout. *Answered 2026-10-27: Alt+5 beyond the list makes a new workspace, as Alt+N does (one new workspace at the end, then switch to it).*
14. **Q14 — DBC "signals in the middle".** D82 describes three areas; the picked prototype B shows two: the outline (nodes → messages → signals) and the inspector, whose centre shows a message's bit layout and signal list. *Recommended: build what the prototype shows (D16).*
15. **Q15 — Does the inspector replace the preview side sheet?** *Recommended: yes from 1200 px window width; below that, Ctrl+Q opens today's side sheet over the other pane, because a third column would leave the panes too narrow.*
16. **Q16 — Which properties are editable in the beta?** *Recommended: the three dates and the four attributes (read-only, hidden, system, archive), on local drives only, applied as one job with Ctrl+Z; owner and permissions stay read only.*
17. **Q17 — Where do thumbnails come from?** *Recommended: Navo's own decoders for images and PDF first pages on every platform, then Windows shell thumbnails for everything else (video, Office) on Windows, in a separate helper process (D88); kept in memory only (64 MB) in the beta.*
18. **Q18 — Tab in Ctrl+K.** Today Tab jumps between result groups; the picked prototype uses Tab for the next mode (Names, Content, Commands, Collections). *Recommended: Tab and Shift+Tab switch modes, as in the prototype; group jumps move to Ctrl+Down and Ctrl+Up.* **Answered 2026-10-28 (owner): Tab and Shift+Tab switch modes; group jumps move to Ctrl+Down and Ctrl+Up.**
19. **Q19 — How does content search read files?** *Recommended: it walks the folders on each search (no content index in the beta); reads UTF-8, UTF-16 with a byte-order mark and Windows-1252; skips binary files (a NUL byte in the first 8 KiB) and files over 64 MB unless `size:` asks for them; `arc:on` looks inside zip, 7z and tar.* **Answered 2026-10-28 (owner): as recommended, walk on each search.**
20. **Q20 — Are collections kept across restarts?** *Recommended: yes, in `navo.db`. A saved search runs again when opened; a snapshot and a hand-made collection keep their list, and missing files show greyed as "missing".*

Find where the vault goes when the volume root is not writable; never in the user's folder, and hidden even when hidden files are shown. | decided 2026-10-29: owner OK — one vault directory per volume, one container per delete; migrate existing sibling containers and `.navo-held-*`; fallback `$XDG_DATA_HOME/navo/vault` (same device), then `<mount>/.Trash-$uid`, then "cannot retain" |
| ST4 | F3 find shows "of N" without the current index and does not highlight hits in Text and Hex. | done 2026-10-29 (previous does not wrap in paged views yet) |
| ST5 | Ctrl+F missing in Table, Tree and Markdown views; typing 1 or 3 there switches the viewer mode. | done 2026-10-29 (a match is a whole cell, node or block) |
| ST6 | Quick filter ignores a leading digit. Cause: the quick filter is a prefix match, so `4999` does not match `file-4999`; digits work as a prefix. Decided 2026-10-29 (owner: "better than TC"): match anywhere in the name, case-insensitive; matched text highlighted; the cursor lands on the first name that starts with the text; a leading `^` restricts to the name start; `*`/`?` work as wildcards. | done 2026-10-29 |
| ST7 | Results tab footer counts do not change after F6 moves files out. | done 2026-10-29 |
| ST8 | Multi-rename leaves a red "Nothing to rename." in the pane footer after a later successful rename. | done 2026-10-28 |
| ST9 | Settings › Display help says Ctrl+L opens the path field (it is Alt+D now). | done 2026-10-28 |
| ST10 | Texts on Linux: "Follow Windows", "System attribute"; Sort by offers "Kind", which the list does not have. | done 2026-10-29 |
| ST11 | Space on a focused dialog button activates it and the rest of the typed text leaks into the quick filter. | open |
| ST12 | Long paths in Copy, Move, Delete, Undo and Recently deleted dialogs are cut at the end: use middle ellipsis keeping the file name and the last folders. | done 2026-10-29 |
| ST13 | F3 archive view: Date column clips the year at 1280 px. F-key bar pills in Tree view and F4 crush to stubs. Popovers (encoding picker, multi-rename tokens) leave the window. Hex ASCII column uses ligatures. | open |
| ST14 | Workspace button sits in the active pane's footer and jumps with focus; it belongs to the window status area. | open, design check |
| ST15 | F4 in the file list asks for an external editor; Enter on a .zip opens it outside Navo (archives as folders is v1.0). Check against decisions before changing. | open, decision check |
| ST16 | Ctrl+Shift+1–4 do nothing on Linux (shifted keysyms). | done 2026-10-29 (US-style symbols; other ASCII layouts not covered) |

**ST3 root cause.** `Vault::capture` creates each container as a sibling of the deleted item, and `validate_container` requires `container.parent() == source.parent()`. No code finds the volume root. Every retained delete leaves one container in the viewed folder until it expires.

**ST3 questions for the owner:**
1. Layout: one vault directory per volume with one container per delete inside it (recommended, smallest change), or one store per volume (catalog format change)?
2. Migration of existing sibling containers and `.navo-held-*` overwrite holders.
3. Fallback when the volume root is not writable: `$XDG_DATA_HOME/navo/vault` on the same device, then `<mount>/.Trash-$uid`, then "This drive cannot keep deleted files".
